HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000745/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0d87b960fd90b63c6d9c","text":"3. **A load-bearing cover that regulates visibility.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `س م و/B004` includes an upper covering surface and fixed `ب ن ي/B009` includes ribs, posts, and settled supports. **Structural cue and role:** the sky is both above the spread ground and the arena for alternation between manifestation and covering; the support image gives the canopy a stabilizing role. **Abductive move:** no supports are stated, so the move from `بناها` to an unseen load-bearing order is explicitly inferred from the construction relation. **Reading change:** the sky reads as a maintained enclosure that makes regulated exposure possible, not a decorative backdrop.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_11c0c36b1373c999be46","text":"3. **Building as kinship/filiation and generated belonging.** Lexical evidence: ب ن ي B007 \"filiation and derived belonging\"; ر ح م B002 \"kinship bond\"; ن ف س B011 \"living soul-self\" in 91:7. Structural cue: after \"what built the sky,\" the packet moves through earth to a fashioned soul. Assigned role: بنى's sonship branch makes created structures belong to their source rather than merely being assembled artifacts. Abductive move: I infer a generated relation between builder and built thing; the packet supplies construction and filiation branches. Reading change: \"what built it\" can be heard as establishing dependence and belonging, preparing \"naqat Allah\" as a creature marked by divine belonging later.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B002","root_000766/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_184c74da086884c35af6","text":"2. **A macro-constitution that activates the self's constitution.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ب ن ي/B002` is built form or constitution; 91:7 `س و ي/B002` is internal straightness and sound completion. **Structural cue and role:** the parallel clauses `وما بناها`, `وما طحاها`, and `وما سواها` move from sky to ground to self, assigning constitution at each scale. **Abductive move:** the parallel grammar is explicit; treating the outer constructions as models for the self's structured capacity is inferred. **Reading change:** the sky-clause becomes the first stage of a nested architecture culminating in moral agency.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000745/B001","root_000745/B004","root_000745/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1addb9c3ff17fa9e18aa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:5:1\",\"root_000745\",[\"B001\",\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Açılıştaki adın yükseltilmesi ile göğün yüksekliği arasındaki bağ, ilahi belirlemeyi kozmik ölçekte görünür bir üst çerçeveye dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2896c3be59e4fd75c50d","text":"91:6-7 completed the unexpected scale sequence: spread ground and proportioned self made `ب ن ي/B002` read as constitution, not only masonry. The divergent outcomes in 91:9-10 then showed why that constitution matters: an assembled capacity can be cultivated or concealed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2f088761ef3581ad1445","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:5:3\",\"root_000156\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kurma eyleminin soy ve aidiyet boyutu, yaratılmış düzeni kaynağına bağlı bir varlık olarak düşünmeye ve ilerideki Allah'ın devesi ifadesine hazırlık yapmaya elverir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_355671645be069c8e34f","text":"The later `ناقة الله` made ب ن ي B007 filiation/belonging feel less optional: built creation, named belonging, messenger of Allah, and she-camel of Allah all form a chain of derived dependence. 91:14 also changed \"what built it\" by contrast: divine building/proportioning is answered by divine re-leveling when the creature marked as belonging to Allah is cut down.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B001","root_000156/B002","root_000745/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6b3464d7e62052abbcfd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:5:1\",\"root_000745\",[\"B004\"]],[\"91:5:3\",\"root_000156\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Göğün kurulması, ışık ve karanlık evrelerini birbirine bağlayan işleyen bir bütünün birleştirilmesini anlatır; bu dış yapı, ileride canın iç yapısına örnek olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000745/B001","root_000745/B004","root_000745/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_83b367435ea7d0fa712d","text":"2. **Name/height resonance binds basmalah and sky.** Lexical evidence: س م و B005 \"name as raised designation\" in 91:0; س م و B001 \"rising high\" and B004 \"overhead sky and cover\" in 91:5; ء ل ه B002 fixed Allah-name. Structural cue: the same root occurs in `بسم` and `السماء`, one at the opening formula and one in the oath-chain. Assigned role: the root س م و bridges linguistic elevation and cosmic elevation. Abductive move: I supply the analogy that naming raises and the sky is raised; the packet supplies the repeated root and branch inventory. Reading change: the ayah makes the opening \"name\" spatially visible: divine designation and cosmic height activate one another.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B009","root_000745/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a5fb8042cb41821661e3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:5:1\",\"root_000745\",[\"B004\"]],[\"91:5:3\",\"root_000156\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Göğün üst örtüsü, görünürlüğün düzenli dönüşümünü taşıyan görünmez desteklere sahip bir yapı gibi tasarlanabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B001","root_000745/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b78a089c4397bedd91e9","text":"1. **Sky as an assembled system.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ب ن ي/B001` is building by joining parts; fixed `س م و/B004` is the overhead sky or upper cover. **Structural cue and role:** the constructed canopy follows four interlocking phases of light and darkness and precedes the spread earth, so building supplies relations and joints rather than a single inert mass. **Abductive move:** the coordinated phases and the verb of building are supplied; taking their fit as evidence of assembly is inferred. **Reading change:** `بناها` reads as integration of a working celestial order, not only erection of height.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B001","root_000156/B002","root_000745/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ca2335550948bb861aa3","text":"1. **Sky as built upper cover, making creation architectural.** Lexical evidence: س م و B004 \"overhead sky and cover\"; ب ن ي B001 \"building by joining parts\"; ب ن ي B002 \"built form and constitution.\" Structural cue: the ayah pairs the upper expanse with \"what built it,\" followed by earth spread out and soul proportioned. Assigned role: سماء supplies vertical covering; بنى supplies constructed joining and constitution. Abductive move: I infer that the cosmic roof models a made order whose parts belong together; the packet supplies upper cover, building, earth spreading, and soul leveling. Reading change: the sky is not just an object sworn by but evidence of constructive composition, later mirrored in the soul's inward formation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000156/B001","root_000156/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f68eb91bdf1b89e938d1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:5:3\",\"root_000156\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kurucu düzen, surenin sonundaki cezalandırıcı düzleştirmeyle karşıtlık kurar: oluşturulan uyum, ağır ihlal karşısında yeniden düzene sokulur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
